<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The working coefficients for components set out in sections 4.1.2.4 and 4.1.2.5 of this Annex are inadequate for machinery intended for the lifting of persons and must, as a general rule, be doubled. Machinery intended for lifting persons or persons and goods must be fitted with a suspension or supporting system for the carrier designed and constructed in such a way as to ensure an adequate overall level of safety and to prevent the risk of the carrier falling.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">If ropes or chains are used to suspend the carrier, as a general rule, at least two independent ropes or chains are required, each with its own anchorage.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.1.2.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Loading control for machinery moved by power other than human strength</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The requirements of section 4.2.2 of this Annex apply regardless of the maximum working load and overturning moment, unless the manufacturer can demonstrate that there is no risk of overloading or overturning.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.2.</td><td colspan="4">CONTROL DEVICES</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">Where safety requirements do not impose other solutions, the carrier must, as a general rule, be designed and constructed in such a way that persons in the carrier have means of controlling upward and downward movements and, if appropriate, other movements of the carrier.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">In operation, those control devices must override any other devices controlling the same movement with the exception of emergency stop devices.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The control devices for these movements must be of the hold-to-run type except where the carrier itself is completely enclosed.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.3.</td><td colspan="4">RISKS TO PERSONS IN OR ON THE CARRIER</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.3.1.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Risks due to movements of the carrier</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">Machinery for lifting persons must be designed, constructed or equipped in such a way that the acceleration or deceleration of the carrier does not engender risks for persons.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.3.2.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Risk of persons falling from the carrier</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The carrier must not tilt to an extent which creates a risk of the occupants falling, including when the machinery and carrier are moving.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">Where the carrier is designed as a work station, provision must be made to ensure stability and to prevent hazardous movements.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">If the measures referred to in section 1.5.15 of this Annex are not adequate, carriers must be fitted with a sufficient number of suitable anchorage points for the number of persons permitted on the carrier. The anchorage points must be strong enough for the use of personal protective equipment against falls from a height.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">Any trapdoor in floors or ceilings or side doors must be designed and constructed in such a way as to prevent inadvertent opening and must open in a direction that obviates any risk of falling, should they open unexpectedly.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.3.3.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Risk due to objects falling on the carrier</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">Where there is a risk of objects falling on the carrier and endangering persons, the carrier must be equipped with a protective roof.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.4.</td><td colspan="4">MACHINERY SERVING FIXED LANDINGS</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.4.1.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Risks to persons in or on the carrier</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The carrier must be designed and constructed in such a way as to prevent risks due to contact between persons and/or objects in or on the carrier with any fixed or moving elements. Where necessary in order to fulfil this requirement, the carrier itself must be completely enclosed with doors fitted with an interlocking device that prevents hazardous movements of the carrier unless the doors are closed. The doors must remain closed if the carrier stops between landings where there is a risk of falling from the carrier.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The machinery must be designed, constructed and, where necessary, equipped with devices in such a way as to prevent uncontrolled upward or downward movement of the carrier. These devices must be able to stop the carrier at its maximum working load and at the foreseeable maximum speed.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The stopping action must not cause deceleration harmful to the occupants, whatever the load conditions.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.4.2.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Controls at landings</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">Controls, other than those for emergency use, at landings must not initiate movements of the carrier when:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="3">the control devices in the carrier are being operated,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="3">the carrier is not at a landing.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.4.3.</td><td colspan="4"><em><strong>Access to the carrier</strong></em></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The guards at the landings and on the carrier must be designed and constructed in such a way as to ensure safe transfer to and from the carrier, taking into consideration the foreseeable range of goods and persons to be lifted.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.5.</td><td colspan="4">MARKINGS</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="4">The carrier must bear the information necessary to ensure safety including:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="3">the number of persons permitted on the carrier,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="3">the maximum working load.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

### PART 3 — Annex III: CE marking (Extent: E+W+S) (affected by repeal, see note) Annex III UK marking (Extent: E+W+S)[^key-e36b13e58a8412d552307137cffa044d]

Regulation 7(2)(f)(ii)

The CE conformity marking shall consist of the initials “CE” taking the following form: *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e36b13e58a8412d552307137cffa044d]

[image: see source](https://www.legislation.gov.uk/uksi/2008/1597/images/uksi_20081597_en_sld_001)

If the CE marking is reduced or enlarged the proportions shown in the above drawing must be respected. *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e36b13e58a8412d552307137cffa044d]

The various components of the CE marking must have substantially the same vertical dimension, which may not be less than 5 mm. The minimum dimension may be waived for small-scale machinery. *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e36b13e58a8412d552307137cffa044d]

The CE marking must be affixed in the immediate vicinity of the name of the responsible person, using the same technique. *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e36b13e58a8412d552307137cffa044d]

Where the full quality assurance procedure prescribed in Annex X (Part 10 of this Schedule) has been applied, the CE marking must be followed by the identification number of the notified body. *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-e36b13e58a8412d552307137cffa044d]

The UK marking must be affixed in the immediate vicinity of the name of the responsible person, using the same technique.

Where the full quality assurance procedure prescribed in Annex X (Part 10 of this Schedule) has been applied, the UK marking must be followed by the identification number of the approved body.

### PART 6 — Annex VI: Assembly instructions for partly completed machinery

Regulation 8(1)(b)

The assembly instructions for partly completed machinery must contain a description of the conditions which must be met with a view to correct incorporation in the final machinery, so as not to compromise safety and health.

The assembly instructions must be written in an official Community *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-8966f95c7646e3e2b81f06eda1e858d4] English or a *(Extent: E+W+S)* language acceptable to the manufacturer of the machinery in which the partly completed machinery will be assembled, or to the manufacturer's authorised representative.

### PART 7 — Annex VII: Technical files

Regulations 7(2)(b) and 8(1)(a)

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><td><strong>A.</strong></td><td colspan="10"><strong>Technical file for machinery</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="10">This part describes the procedure for compiling a technical file. The technical file must demonstrate that the machinery complies with the provisions of the Directive <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>. It must cover the design, manufacture and operation of the machinery to the extent necessary for this assessment. The technical file must be compiled in one or more official Community languages <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> English, except for the instructions for the machinery, for which the special provisions of Annex I (Part 1 of this Schedule), section 1.7.4.1 apply.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="3">1.</td><td colspan="7">The technical file shall comprise the following:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">(a)</td><td colspan="4">a construction file including:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">a general description of the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">the overall drawing of the machinery and drawings of the control circuits, as well as the pertinent descriptions and explanations necessary for understanding the operation of the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">full detailed drawings, accompanied by any calculation notes, test results, certificates, etc., required to check the conformity of the machinery with the essential health and safety requirements,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">the documentation on risk assessment demonstrating the procedure followed, including:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="10">(i)</td><td>a list of the essential health and safety requirements which apply to the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="10">(ii)</td><td>the description of the protective measures implemented to eliminate identified hazards or to reduce risks and, when appropriate, the indication of the residual risks associated with the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">the standards and other technical specifications used, indicating the essential health and safety requirements covered by these standards,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">any technical report giving the results of the tests carried out either by the manufacturer or by a body chosen by the responsible person,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">a copy of the instructions for the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">where appropriate, the declaration of incorporation for included partly completed machinery and the relevant assembly instructions for such machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">where appropriate, copies of the EC <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> declaration of conformity of machinery or other products incorporated into the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="8"></td><td colspan="3">a copy of the EC <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> declaration of conformity;</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">(b)</td><td colspan="4">for series manufacture, the internal measures that will be implemented to ensure that the machinery remains in conformity with the provisions of the Directive <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4"></td><td colspan="7">The manufacturer must carry out necessary research and tests on components, fittings or the completed machinery to determine whether by its design or construction it is capable of being assembled and put into service safely. The relevant reports and results shall be included in the technical file.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="3">2.</td><td colspan="7">The technical file referred to in point 1 of this Annex must be made available to the enforcement authorities and the competent authorities of any <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> other relevant <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> state <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> for at least 10 years following the date of manufacture of the machinery or, in the case of series manufacture, of the last unit produced.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4"></td><td colspan="7">The technical file does not have to be located in the territory of <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> a relevant <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> state <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> United Kingdom, nor does it have to be permanently available in material form. However, it must be capable of being assembled and made available within a period of time commensurate with its complexity by the person designated in the EC <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> declaration of conformity.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="4"></td><td colspan="7">The technical file does not have to include detailed plans or any other specific information as regards the sub-assemblies used for the manufacture of the machinery unless a knowledge of them is essential for verification of conformity with the essential health and safety requirements.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="3">3.</td><td colspan="7">Failure to present the technical file in response to a duly reasoned request by the competent national authorities <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> enforcement authorities may constitute sufficient grounds for doubting the conformity of the machinery in question with the essential health and safety requirements.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td><strong>B.</strong></td><td colspan="10"><strong>Relevant technical documentation for partly completed machinery</strong></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="10">This part describes the procedure for compiling relevant technical documentation. The documentation must show which provisions of the Directive <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> are applied and fulfilled. It must cover the design, manufacture and operation of the partly completed machinery to the extent necessary for the assessment of conformity with the essential health and safety requirements applied. The documentation must be compiled in one or more official Community languages <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> English <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="10">Relevant technical documentation shall comprise the following:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3">(a)</td><td colspan="8">a construction file including:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="6"></td><td colspan="5">the overall drawing of the partly completed machinery and drawings of the control circuits,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="5"></td><td colspan="6">full detailed drawings, accompanied by any calculation notes, test results, certificates, etc., required to check the conformity of the partly completed machinery with the applied essential health and safety requirements,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="6"></td><td colspan="5">the risk assessment documentation showing the procedure followed, including:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="9">(i)</td><td colspan="2">a list of the essential health and safety requirements applied and fulfilled,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="9">(ii)</td><td colspan="2">the description of the protective measures implemented to eliminate identified hazards or to reduce risks and, where appropriate, the indication of the residual risks,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="9">(iii)</td><td colspan="2">the standards and other technical specifications used, indicating the essential health and safety requirements covered by these standards,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="9">(iv)</td><td colspan="2">any technical report giving the results of the tests carried out either by the manufacturer or by a body chosen by the responsible person,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="9">(v)</td><td colspan="2">a copy of the assembly instructions for the partly completed machinery;</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3">(b)</td><td colspan="8">for series manufacture, the internal measures that will be implemented to ensure that the partly completed machinery remains in conformity with the essential health and safety requirements applied.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="9">The manufacturer must carry out necessary research and tests on components, fittings or the partly completed machinery to determine whether by its design or construction it is capable of being assembled and used safely. The relevant reports and results shall be included in the technical file.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="9">The relevant technical documentation must be available for at least 10 years following the date of manufacture of the partly completed machinery or, in the case of series manufacture, of the last unit produced, and on request presented to an enforcement authority or a competent authority of any <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> other relevant <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> state <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em>. It does not have to be located in the territory of <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> a relevant <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> state <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> United Kingdom <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>, nor does it have to be permanently available in material form. It must be capable of being assembled and presented to the relevant authority by the person designated in the declaration for incorporation.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="11"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td colspan="9">Failure to present the relevant technical documentation in response to a duly reasoned request by an enforcement authority or a competent authority of any <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> other relevant <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> state <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> may constitute sufficient grounds for doubting the conformity of the partly completed machinery with the essential health and safety requirements applied and attested.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^key-8ee355b7ac56185df296cebb7486d45f][^key-b75cdccd63b9ab7de4f8f7f2501c4386][^key-f02e931b637a036aca30933c912f4db2][^key-f02e931b637a036aca30933c912f4db2][^key-ee2d2871e45cd8a4e6310c818bb88379][^key-76b47f89379a22df0b40de9a9fec6101][^key-02f1b94112a84693008e714496055d8d][^key-76b47f89379a22df0b40de9a9fec6101][^key-76b47f89379a22df0b40de9a9fec6101][^key-c685c3e2643fd933bfb0c526ab8929ad][^key-44b9ee2784470e53fc2e528eaefbe70d][^key-c685c3e2643fd933bfb0c526ab8929ad][^key-c685c3e2643fd933bfb0c526ab8929ad][^key-09558c10d04d35f49c8e4d562cb60b62][^key-fdc4a72ea40b2638ab548deaf1692247][^key-8cdc6ecb59f6e9567ee1d8e664668678][^key-19f266929f526a84eb5f1da944cb610a][^key-fd4da45ecf8a1c090b66ccbb05a4c5dd][^key-fd4da45ecf8a1c090b66ccbb05a4c5dd][^key-fd4da45ecf8a1c090b66ccbb05a4c5dd][^key-21b1cb3999061ca1384f22a5c1960d8f][^key-21b1cb3999061ca1384f22a5c1960d8f][^key-21b1cb3999061ca1384f22a5c1960d8f][^key-fd4da45ecf8a1c090b66ccbb05a4c5dd][^key-fd4da45ecf8a1c090b66ccbb05a4c5dd][^key-fd4da45ecf8a1c090b66ccbb05a4c5dd]

### PART 8 — Annex VIII: Assessment of conformity with internal checks on the manufacture of machinery

Regulations 10, 11(2), 12(2)(a)

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><td>1.</td><td>This Annex describes the procedure by which the responsible person, who carries out the obligations laid down in points 2 and 3 of this Annex, ensures and declares that the machinery concerned satisfies the relevant provisions of the Directive <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.</td><td>For each representative type of the series in question, the responsible person shall draw up the technical file referred to in Annex VII (Part 7 of this Schedule), part A.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.</td><td>The manufacturer must take all measures necessary in order that the manufacturing process ensures compliance of the manufactured machinery with the technical file referred to in Annex VII, part A, and with the provisions of the Directive <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^key-7ed129eb1dc4b45ae689c076e726e84a][^key-7ed129eb1dc4b45ae689c076e726e84a]

### PART 9 — Annex IX: EC type- (affected by repeal, see note) Type- examination[^key-6d3b5e91e82852013c8e87f9e626a6c4]

*Extent: E+W+S*

Regulations 11(2)(b), 12(2)(a), 18(1)

EC type- *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-5fb4d9c04bc2b682a20200740b6ab9e0] Type- examination is the procedure whereby a notified *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-d26a2ba767bb0592a89694e0479054c1] an approved body ascertains and certifies that a representative model of machinery referred to in Annex IV (Part 4 of this Schedule) (hereafter named the type) satisfies the provisions of the Directive *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-546ed9596185d38482dab0f8512d4842] these Regulations.

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><td>1.</td><td colspan="2">The responsible person must, for each type, draw up the technical file referred to in Annex VII (Part 7 of this Schedule), part A.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.</td><td colspan="2">For each type, the application for an EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> a Type- examination shall be submitted by the responsible person to a notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> an approved body chosen by the responsible person.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The application shall include:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the name and address of the manufacturer and, where appropriate, the manufacturer's authorised representative,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>a written declaration that the application has not been submitted to another notified body,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the technical file.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">Moreover, the applicant shall place at the disposal of the notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body a sample of the type. The notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body may ask for further samples if the test programme so requires.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.</td><td colspan="2">The notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body shall:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.1.</td><td colspan="2">examine the technical file, check that the type was manufactured in accordance with it and establish which elements have been designed in accordance with the relevant provisions of published harmonised standards, and those elements whose design is not based on the relevant provisions of those standards;</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.2.</td><td colspan="2">carry out or have carried out appropriate inspections, measurements and tests to ascertain whether the solutions adopted satisfy the essential health and safety requirements, where published harmonised <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> designated standards were not applied;</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.3.</td><td colspan="2">where published harmonised <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> designated standards were used, carry out or have carried out appropriate inspections, measurements and tests to verify that those standards were actually applied;</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.4.</td><td colspan="2">agree with the applicant as to the place where the check that the type was manufactured in accordance with the examined technical file and the necessary inspections, measurements and tests will be carried out.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.</td><td colspan="2">If the type satisfies the provisions of the Directive <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations, the notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body shall issue the applicant with an EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> a Type- examination certificate. The certificate shall include the name and address of the manufacturer and the manufacturer's authorised representative, the data necessary for identifying the approved type, the conclusions of the examination and the conditions to which its issue may be subject.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer and the notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body shall retain a copy of this certificate, the technical file and all relevant documents for a period of 15 years from the date of issue of the certificate.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>5.</td><td colspan="2">If the type does not satisfy the provisions of the Directive, the notified body shall refuse to issue the applicant with an EC type-examination certificate, giving detailed reasons for its refusal. It shall inform the applicant, the other notified bodies and the Member State which notified it. An appeal procedure must be available. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> If the type does not satisfy the provisions of these Regulations, the approved body shall refuse to issue the applicant with a Type-examination certificate, giving detailed reasons for its refusal. It shall inform the applicant, the other approved bodies and the Secretary of State. An appeal procedure must be available.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.</td><td colspan="2">The applicant shall inform the notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body which retains the technical file relating to the EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificate of all modifications to the approved type. The notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body shall examine these modifications and shall then either confirm the validity of the existing EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificate or issue a new one if the modifications are liable to compromise conformity with the essential health and safety requirements or the intended working conditions of the type.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>7.</td><td colspan="2">The Commission, the Member States <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Secretary of State and the other notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved bodies may, on request, obtain a copy of the EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificates. On reasoned request, the Commission and the Member States <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Secretary of State may obtain a copy of the technical file and the results of the examinations carried out by the notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>8.</td><td colspan="2">Files and correspondence referring to the EC type-examination procedures shall be written in the official language(s) of the <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> relevant <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> state where the notified body is established or in any other official Community language acceptable to the notified body. <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Files and correspondence referring to the Type-examination procedures shall be written in English or any other language acceptable to the approved body.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.</td><td colspan="2">Validity of the EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificate</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.1.</td><td colspan="2">The notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body has the ongoing responsibility of ensuring that the EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificate remains valid. It shall inform the manufacturer of any major changes which would have an implication on the validity of the certificate. The notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body shall withdraw certificates which are no longer valid.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.2.</td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer of the machinery concerned has the ongoing responsibility of ensuring that the said machinery meets the corresponding state of the art.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.3.</td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer shall request from the notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved body the review of the validity of the EC type- <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificate every five years.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">If the notified body finds that the certificate remains valid, taking into account the state of the art, it shall renew the certificate for a further five years.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer and the notified body shall retain a copy of this certificate, of the technical file and of all the relevant documents for a period of 15 years from the date of issue of the certificate.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>9.4.</td><td colspan="2">In the event that the validity of the EC-type <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> Type- examination certificate is not renewed, the manufacturer shall cease the placing on the market of the machinery concerned.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^key-bb68c2eeb2b300d4641a134b5f84f20c][^key-faf9a4a36817919902b10cb6cd0c39c0][^key-4f143233507fcd1f40fc31d4e718ff5c][^key-4f143233507fcd1f40fc31d4e718ff5c][^key-8fdef2855d6951a999f8bc4cf46c6cad][^key-772b02f469dabbfa367e3b714302c8cb][^key-772b02f469dabbfa367e3b714302c8cb][^key-06534d1b05f930d1f7e0adae4dce278c][^key-e2673e351d615c2b90b4ef1a7f83c8b7][^key-3c3485c6bf74916355330a629fc770d1][^key-e2673e351d615c2b90b4ef1a7f83c8b7][^key-c49f2812ab5e0d2b35c40a02c95a29bc][^key-8f10289102056d7c8459374b71cbdd56][^key-f3f382ca51f4a6e2fae70d3f66b7e79c][^key-8f10289102056d7c8459374b71cbdd56][^key-f3f382ca51f4a6e2fae70d3f66b7e79c][^key-b7caa0aacf5220d3f73b40fa446129de][^key-ef8da8e395caf898636d7f49156f1552][^key-a3bff19f0d8064c411ff276096ae8e76][^key-6b4fba3b2ee6e876bfb53761f9f0d86b][^key-ef8da8e395caf898636d7f49156f1552][^key-f446a65bfcf925f5f90a231920b07b78][^key-76490dc59bcdec11086bd09b519c057e][^key-f446a65bfcf925f5f90a231920b07b78][^key-f446a65bfcf925f5f90a231920b07b78][^key-3ff7c57a9b71c16fbb8ab86b18085278][^key-ded9ff0e9b287f03aa0f6e66a3edb756][^key-3ff7c57a9b71c16fbb8ab86b18085278][^key-ded9ff0e9b287f03aa0f6e66a3edb756][^key-ded9ff0e9b287f03aa0f6e66a3edb756][^key-3ff7c57a9b71c16fbb8ab86b18085278][^key-3ff7c57a9b71c16fbb8ab86b18085278]

### PART 10 — Annex X: Full quality assurance

Regulations 11(2)(c), 12(2)(b), 18(1)

This Annex describes the conformity assessment of machinery referred to in Annex IV (Part 4 of this Schedule), manufactured using a full quality assurance system, and the procedure whereby a notified *(Extent: E+W+S)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^key-abb2e79babfdb3e903a362a99fb0bbe2] an approved *(Extent: E+W+S)* body assesses and approves the quality system and monitors its application.

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><td>1.</td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer must operate an approved quality system for design, manufacture, final inspection and testing, as specified in point 2 of this Annex, and shall be subject to the surveillance referred to in point 3 of this Annex.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.</td><td colspan="2">Quality system</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.1.</td><td colspan="2">An application for assessment of a quality system shall be lodged by the responsible person with a notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> an approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body chosen by the responsible person.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The application shall contain:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the name and address of the manufacturer and, where appropriate, the manufacturer's authorised representative,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the places of design, manufacture, inspection, testing and storage of the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the technical file described in Annex VII (Part 7 of this Schedule), part A, for one model of each category of machinery referred to in Annex IV (Part 4 of this Schedule) which the manufacturer intends to manufacture,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the documentation on the quality system,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>a written declaration that the application has not been submitted to another notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.2.</td><td colspan="2">The quality system must ensure conformity of the machinery with the provisions of the Directive <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>. All the elements, requirements and provisions adopted by the manufacturer must be documented in a systematic and orderly manner, in the form of measures, procedures and written instructions. The documentation on the quality system must permit a uniform interpretation of the procedural and quality measures, such as quality programmes, plans, manuals and records.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">It must contain, in particular, an adequate description of:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the quality objectives, the organisational structure, and the responsibilities and powers of the management with regard to the design and quality of the machinery,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the technical design specifications, including standards that will be applied and, where published harmonised <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> designated <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> standards are not applied in full, the means that will be used to ensure that the essential health and safety requirements are fulfilled,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the design inspection and design verification techniques, processes and systematic actions that will be used when designing machinery covered by the Directive <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> these Regulations <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em>,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the corresponding manufacturing, quality control and quality assurance techniques, processes and systematic actions that will be used,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the inspections and tests that will be carried out before, during and after manufacture, and the frequency with which they will be carried out,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the quality records, such as inspection reports and test data, calibration data, and reports on the qualifications of the personnel concerned,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the means of monitoring the achievement of the required design and quality of the machinery, as well as the effective operation of the quality system.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.3.</td><td colspan="2">The notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body shall assess the quality system to determine whether it satisfies the requirements of point 2.2 of this Annex.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The elements of the quality system which conform to the relevant harmonised <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> designated <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> standard shall be presumed to conform to the corresponding requirements referred to in point 2.2.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The team of auditors must have at least one member who is experienced in the assessment of the technology of the machinery. The assessment procedure shall include an inspection to be carried out at the manufacturer's premises. During the assessment, the team of auditors shall carry out a review of the technical files referred to in the third indent of the second paragraph of point 2.1 of this Annex, to ensure their compliance with the applicable health and safety requirements.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The responsible person shall be notified of the decision. The notification shall contain the conclusions of the examination and the reasoned assessment decision. An appeal procedure must be available.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.4.</td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer shall undertake to fulfil the obligations arising from the quality system as approved and to ensure that it remains appropriate and effective.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The responsible person shall inform the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body which approved the quality system of any planned change to it.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">The notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body shall evaluate the proposed changes and decide whether the modified quality assurance system will continue to satisfy the requirements referred to in point 2.2, or whether a re-assessment is necessary.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">It shall notify the manufacturer of its decision. The notification shall contain the conclusions of the examination and the reasoned assessment decision.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.</td><td colspan="2">Surveillance under the responsibility of the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.1.</td><td colspan="2">The purpose of surveillance is to make sure that the manufacturer duly fulfils the obligations arising out of the approved quality system.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.2.</td><td colspan="2">The manufacturer shall, for inspection purposes, allow the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body access to the places of design, manufacture, inspection, testing and storage, and shall provide it with all necessary information, such as:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the documentation concerning the quality system,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the quality records provided for in that part of the quality system concerned with design, such as the results of analyses, calculations, tests, etc.,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the quality records provided for in that part of the quality system concerned with manufacture, such as inspection reports and test data, calibration data, reports on the qualifications of the personnel concerned, etc.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.3.</td><td colspan="2">The notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body shall conduct periodic audits to make sure that the manufacturer is maintaining and applying the quality system; it shall provide the manufacturer with an audit report. The frequency of the periodic audits shall be such that a full reassessment is carried out every three years.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.4.</td><td colspan="2">Moreover, the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body may pay the manufacturer unannounced visits. The need for these additional visits and their frequency will be determined on the basis of a visit monitoring system managed by the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body. In particular, the following factors will be taken into account in the visits monitoring system:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the results of previous surveillance visits,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the need to monitor remedial measures,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>where appropriate, special conditions attaching to approval of the system,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>significant modifications in the organisation of the manufacturing process, measures or techniques.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td></td><td colspan="2">On the occasion of such visits, the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body may, if necessary, carry out tests or have them carried out in order to check the proper functioning of the quality system. It shall provide the manufacturer with a visit report and, if a test was carried out, with a test report.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.</td><td colspan="2">The responsible person shall keep available for the national authorities, for a period of ten years from the last date of manufacture:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the documentation referred to in point 2.1 of this Annex,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the decisions and reports of the notified <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved <em>(Extent: E+W+S)</em> body referred to in the third and fourth subparagraphs of point 2.4 of this Annex, and in points 3.3 and 3.4 of this Annex.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^key-abb2e79babfdb3e903a362a99fb0bbe2][^key-0e05f4cf6e18ec777b3be120e2281a3d][^key-91d5569326d79216d081607e7a103673][^key-3f288e0b58ab8813aefa50d2a8f53821][^key-91d5569326d79216d081607e7a103673][^key-c0c7b491d9d899d0077bd80fc6ea4dc8][^key-3f288e0b58ab8813aefa50d2a8f53821][^key-1c469158af14cdfb8395b342678e53b5][^key-1c469158af14cdfb8395b342678e53b5][^key-bf504f67e36f5dfd0664d40842ab7151][^key-91d8c9e1974f9db040db52c7242c4366][^key-59b9b3a6a3fe969414dd2208198d6916][^key-a19d24b9e695d3f2cc9bc512f00182ac][^key-a19d24b9e695d3f2cc9bc512f00182ac][^key-a19d24b9e695d3f2cc9bc512f00182ac][^key-bfe315d74cae9bcda0682ace00d70926]

### PART 11 — Annex XI: Minimum criteria to be taken into account by Member States for the notification (affected by repeal, see note) the Secretary of State for approval of bodies[^key-db5ed3395f29aac25ef3ac5a589e3070]

*Extent: E+W+S*

Regulation 16(6)

<table>
<tbody>
<tr><td>1.</td><td colspan="2">The body, its director and the staff responsible for carrying out the verification tests shall not be the designer, manufacturer, supplier or installer of machines which they inspect, nor the authorised representative of any of these parties. They shall not become involved, either directly or as authorised representatives, in the design, construction, marketing or maintenance of the machines. This does not preclude the possibility of exchanges of technical information between the manufacturer and the body.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>2.</td><td colspan="2">The body and its staff shall carry out the verification tests with the highest degree of professional integrity and technical competence and shall be free from all pressures and inducements, particularly financial, which might influence their judgement or the results of the inspection, especially from persons or groups of persons with an interest in the result of verifications.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>3.</td><td colspan="2">For each category of machinery for which it is notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved, the body must possess personnel with technical knowledge and sufficient and appropriate experience to perform a conformity assessment. It must have the means necessary to complete the technical and administrative tasks connected with implementation of the checks in an appropriate manner; it must also have access to the equipment necessary for the exceptional checks.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>4.</td><td colspan="2">The staff responsible for inspection shall have:</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>sound technical and vocational training,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>satisfactory knowledge of the requirements of the tests they carry out and adequate experience of such tests,</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="2"></td><td>the ability to draw up the certificates, records and reports required to authenticate the performance of the tests.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>5.</td><td colspan="2">The impartiality of inspection staff shall be guaranteed. Their remuneration shall not depend on the number of tests carried out or on the results of such tests.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>6.</td><td colspan="2">The body shall satisfy the Secretary of State that it has adequate civil liability insurance.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>7.</td><td colspan="2">The staff of the body shall be bound to observe professional secrecy with regard to all information obtained in carrying out its tasks (except vis-à-vis the Secretary of State) under these Regulations.</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="3"></td></tr>
<tr><td>8.</td><td colspan="2">Notified <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> approved bodies shall participate in coordination activities. They shall also take part directly or be represented in European standardisation, or <em>(affected by repeal, see note)</em> ensure that they know the situation in respect of relevant standards.</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

[^key-516e9283f5fcf241f46e8bd6d12e9258][^key-4b19b62b3a98c75413c237b3b59ce5d1][^key-948fdcf53adb6cd3a2e6ccdc2f4f8543]
